<p style="margin-bottom:1.5rem"><span><span data-word-idx="36" class="tts-word">Silopi</span> <span data-word-idx="37" class="tts-word">Dayanışma</span> <span data-word-idx="38" class="tts-word">İnisiyatifi,</span> <span data-word-idx="39" class="tts-word">DEM</span> <span data-word-idx="40" class="tts-word">Parti</span> <span data-word-idx="41" class="tts-word">tarafından</span> <span data-word-idx="42" class="tts-word">&ldquo;kesin</span> <span data-word-idx="43" class="tts-word">ihra&ccedil;&rdquo;</span> <span data-word-idx="44" class="tts-word">talebiyle</span> <span data-word-idx="45" class="tts-word">disiplin</span> <span data-word-idx="46" class="tts-word">kuruluna</span> <span data-word-idx="47" class="tts-word">sevk</span> <span data-word-idx="48" class="tts-word">edilen</span> <span data-word-idx="49" class="tts-word">ve</span> <span data-word-idx="50" class="tts-word">hakkında</span> <span data-word-idx="51" class="tts-word">istifa</span> <span data-word-idx="52" class="tts-word">&ccedil;ağrıları</span> <span data-word-idx="53" class="tts-word">yapılan</span> <span data-word-idx="54" class="tts-word">Silopi</span> <span data-word-idx="55" class="tts-word">Belediye</span> <span data-word-idx="56" class="tts-word">Eşbaşkanı</span> <span data-word-idx="57" class="tts-word">Av.</span> <span data-word-idx="58" class="tts-word">Jiyan</span> <span data-word-idx="59" class="tts-word">Ormanlı&rsquo;ya</span> <span data-word-idx="60" class="tts-word">y&ouml;nelik</span> <span data-word-idx="61" class="tts-word">baskılara</span> <span data-word-idx="62" class="tts-word">ilişkin</span> <span data-word-idx="63" class="tts-word">yazılı</span> <span data-word-idx="64" class="tts-word">bir</span> <span data-word-idx="65" class="tts-word">a&ccedil;ıklama</span> <span data-word-idx="66" class="tts-word">yayımladı.</span> </span></p>
<p style="margin-bottom:1.5rem"><span><span data-word-idx="67" class="tts-word">A&ccedil;ıklamada,</span> <span data-word-idx="68" class="tts-word">&ldquo;Demokrasi,</span> <span data-word-idx="69" class="tts-word">halkın</span> <span data-word-idx="70" class="tts-word">en</span> <span data-word-idx="71" class="tts-word">kutsal</span> <span data-word-idx="72" class="tts-word">hakkıdır.</span> <span data-word-idx="73" class="tts-word">Bu</span> <span data-word-idx="74" class="tts-word">hakkın</span> <span data-word-idx="75" class="tts-word">teminatı</span> <span data-word-idx="76" class="tts-word">ise</span> <span data-word-idx="77" class="tts-word">&ouml;zg&uuml;r</span> <span data-word-idx="78" class="tts-word">ve</span> <span data-word-idx="79" class="tts-word">adil</span> <span data-word-idx="80" class="tts-word">se&ccedil;imlerdir.</span> <span data-word-idx="81" class="tts-word">Se&ccedil;imle</span> <span data-word-idx="82" class="tts-word">gelen</span> <span data-word-idx="83" class="tts-word">ancak</span> <span data-word-idx="84" class="tts-word">se&ccedil;imle</span> <span data-word-idx="85" class="tts-word">gider&rdquo;</span> <span data-word-idx="86" class="tts-word">ifadeleriyle,</span> <span data-word-idx="87" class="tts-word">Jiyan</span> <span data-word-idx="88" class="tts-word">Ormanlı&rsquo;nın</span> <span data-word-idx="89" class="tts-word">g&ouml;revde</span> <span data-word-idx="90" class="tts-word">kalmasının</span> <span data-word-idx="91" class="tts-word">halk</span> <span data-word-idx="92" class="tts-word">iradesinin</span> <span data-word-idx="93" class="tts-word">gereği</span> <span data-word-idx="94" class="tts-word">olduğu</span> <span data-word-idx="95" class="tts-word">vurgulandı.</span></span></p>
<p style="margin-bottom:1.5rem"><strong><span><span data-word-idx="96" class="tts-word">&nbsp;&ldquo;Demokrasiye</span> <span data-word-idx="97" class="tts-word">m&uuml;dahale</span> <span data-word-idx="98" class="tts-word">kabul</span> <span data-word-idx="99" class="tts-word">edilemez&rdquo;</span></span></strong></p>
<p style="margin-bottom:1.5rem"><span><span data-word-idx="100" class="tts-word">Silopi</span> <span data-word-idx="101" class="tts-word">Dayanışma</span> <span data-word-idx="102" class="tts-word">İnisiyatifi&rsquo;nin</span> <span data-word-idx="103" class="tts-word">a&ccedil;ıklamasında,</span> <span data-word-idx="104" class="tts-word">&ldquo;Ge&ccedil;mişte</span> <span data-word-idx="105" class="tts-word">kayyumlara</span> <span data-word-idx="106" class="tts-word">karşı</span> <span data-word-idx="107" class="tts-word">duranların</span> <span data-word-idx="108" class="tts-word">bug&uuml;n</span> <span data-word-idx="109" class="tts-word">benzer</span> <span data-word-idx="110" class="tts-word">y&ouml;ntemlere</span> <span data-word-idx="111" class="tts-word">y&ouml;nelmesi</span> <span data-word-idx="112" class="tts-word">kabul</span> <span data-word-idx="113" class="tts-word">edilemez</span> <span data-word-idx="114" class="tts-word">bir</span> <span data-word-idx="115" class="tts-word">&ccedil;ifte</span> <span data-word-idx="116" class="tts-word">standarttır&rdquo;</span> <span data-word-idx="117" class="tts-word">denildi.</span> <span data-word-idx="118" class="tts-word">A&ccedil;ıklamada</span> <span data-word-idx="119" class="tts-word">şu</span> <span data-word-idx="120" class="tts-word">ifadelere</span> <span data-word-idx="121" class="tts-word">yer</span> <span data-word-idx="122" class="tts-word">verildi:</span></span></p>
<p style="margin-bottom:1.5rem"><span><span data-word-idx="123" class="tts-word">&nbsp;&ldquo;Sayın</span> <span data-word-idx="124" class="tts-word">Jiyan</span> <span data-word-idx="125" class="tts-word">Ormanlı</span> <span data-word-idx="126" class="tts-word">hakkında</span> <span data-word-idx="127" class="tts-word">dile</span> <span data-word-idx="128" class="tts-word">getirilen</span> <span data-word-idx="129" class="tts-word">iddialar</span> <span data-word-idx="130" class="tts-word">varsa,</span> <span data-word-idx="131" class="tts-word">hukuk</span> <span data-word-idx="132" class="tts-word">&ccedil;er&ccedil;evesinde</span> <span data-word-idx="133" class="tts-word">değerlendirilmelidir.</span> <span data-word-idx="134" class="tts-word">Ancak</span> <span data-word-idx="135" class="tts-word">hi&ccedil;bir</span> <span data-word-idx="136" class="tts-word">parti</span> <span data-word-idx="137" class="tts-word">kararı</span> <span data-word-idx="138" class="tts-word">ya</span> <span data-word-idx="139" class="tts-word">da</span> <span data-word-idx="140" class="tts-word">dış</span> <span data-word-idx="141" class="tts-word">baskı,</span> <span data-word-idx="142" class="tts-word">halkın</span> <span data-word-idx="143" class="tts-word">se&ccedil;ilmiş</span> <span data-word-idx="144" class="tts-word">temsilcisinin</span> <span data-word-idx="145" class="tts-word">g&ouml;revini</span> <span data-word-idx="146" class="tts-word">yapmasını</span> <span data-word-idx="147" class="tts-word">engelleyemez.&rdquo;</span></span></p>
<p style="margin-bottom:1.5rem"><span><span data-word-idx="148" class="tts-word">Belediyelerin</span> <span data-word-idx="149" class="tts-word">kişisel</span> <span data-word-idx="150" class="tts-word">ya</span> <span data-word-idx="151" class="tts-word">da</span> <span data-word-idx="152" class="tts-word">siyasi</span> <span data-word-idx="153" class="tts-word">rant</span> <span data-word-idx="154" class="tts-word">alanı</span> <span data-word-idx="155" class="tts-word">olmadığının</span> <span data-word-idx="156" class="tts-word">altı</span> <span data-word-idx="157" class="tts-word">&ccedil;izilen</span> <span data-word-idx="158" class="tts-word">a&ccedil;ıklamada,</span> <span data-word-idx="159" class="tts-word">&ldquo;Belediyeler</span> <span data-word-idx="160" class="tts-word">halkın</span> <span data-word-idx="161" class="tts-word">ortak</span> <span data-word-idx="162" class="tts-word">iradesinin</span> <span data-word-idx="163" class="tts-word">kurumlaşmış</span> <span data-word-idx="164" class="tts-word">halidir.</span> <span data-word-idx="165" class="tts-word">Bu</span> <span data-word-idx="166" class="tts-word">ger&ccedil;eği</span> <span data-word-idx="167" class="tts-word">yok</span> <span data-word-idx="168" class="tts-word">sayan</span> <span data-word-idx="169" class="tts-word">her</span> <span data-word-idx="170" class="tts-word">girişim,</span> <span data-word-idx="171" class="tts-word">demokrasiye</span> <span data-word-idx="172" class="tts-word">a&ccedil;ık</span> <span data-word-idx="173" class="tts-word">saldırıdır&rdquo;</span> <span data-word-idx="174" class="tts-word">ifadeleri</span> <span data-word-idx="175" class="tts-word">kullanıldı.</span></span></p>
<p style="margin-bottom:1.5rem"><strong><span><span data-word-idx="176" class="tts-word">&ldquo;İstifa</span> <span data-word-idx="177" class="tts-word">talepleri</span> <span data-word-idx="178" class="tts-word">halkın</span> <span data-word-idx="179" class="tts-word">onuruna</span> <span data-word-idx="180" class="tts-word">darbedir&rdquo;</span></span></strong></p>
<p style="margin-bottom:1.5rem"><span><span data-word-idx="181" class="tts-word">Son</span> <span data-word-idx="182" class="tts-word">g&uuml;nlerde</span> <span data-word-idx="183" class="tts-word">bazı</span> <span data-word-idx="184" class="tts-word">gruplar</span> <span data-word-idx="185" class="tts-word">tarafından</span> <span data-word-idx="186" class="tts-word">belediye</span> <span data-word-idx="187" class="tts-word">&ouml;n&uuml;nde</span> <span data-word-idx="188" class="tts-word">yapılan</span> <span data-word-idx="189" class="tts-word">istifa</span> <span data-word-idx="190" class="tts-word">&ccedil;ağrılı</span> <span data-word-idx="191" class="tts-word">protestoları</span> <span data-word-idx="192" class="tts-word">da</span> <span data-word-idx="193" class="tts-word">eleştiren</span> <span data-word-idx="194" class="tts-word">İnisiyatif,</span> <span data-word-idx="195" class="tts-word">bu</span> <span data-word-idx="196" class="tts-word">girişimleri</span> <span data-word-idx="197" class="tts-word">&ldquo;sinsi</span> <span data-word-idx="198" class="tts-word">ve</span> <span data-word-idx="199" class="tts-word">tehlikeli</span> <span data-word-idx="200" class="tts-word">m&uuml;dahale&rdquo;</span> <span data-word-idx="201" class="tts-word">olarak</span> <span data-word-idx="202" class="tts-word">niteledi:</span></span></p>
<p style="margin-bottom:1.5rem"><span><span data-word-idx="203" class="tts-word">&nbsp;&ldquo;Demokrasi</span> <span data-word-idx="204" class="tts-word">sokakta</span> <span data-word-idx="205" class="tts-word">değil,</span> <span data-word-idx="206" class="tts-word">sandıkta</span> <span data-word-idx="207" class="tts-word">şekillenir.</span> <span data-word-idx="208" class="tts-word">Hi&ccedil;</span> <span data-word-idx="209" class="tts-word">kimse,</span> <span data-word-idx="210" class="tts-word">halk</span> <span data-word-idx="211" class="tts-word">adına</span> <span data-word-idx="212" class="tts-word">karar</span> <span data-word-idx="213" class="tts-word">alma</span> <span data-word-idx="214" class="tts-word">yetkisini</span> <span data-word-idx="215" class="tts-word">kendinde</span> <span data-word-idx="216" class="tts-word">g&ouml;remez.</span> <span data-word-idx="217" class="tts-word">Bu</span> <span data-word-idx="218" class="tts-word">t&uuml;r</span> <span data-word-idx="219" class="tts-word">dayatmalar,</span> <span data-word-idx="220" class="tts-word">Silopi</span> <span data-word-idx="221" class="tts-word">halkının</span> <span data-word-idx="222" class="tts-word">&ouml;zg&uuml;r</span> <span data-word-idx="223" class="tts-word">iradesine</span> <span data-word-idx="224" class="tts-word">ve</span> <span data-word-idx="225" class="tts-word">demokrasi</span> <span data-word-idx="226" class="tts-word">k&uuml;lt&uuml;r&uuml;ne</span> <span data-word-idx="227" class="tts-word">hakarettir.&rdquo;</span></span></p>
<p style="margin-bottom:1.5rem"><strong><span><span data-word-idx="228" class="tts-word">&ldquo;Silopi&rsquo;nin</span> <span data-word-idx="229" class="tts-word">onuru</span> <span data-word-idx="230" class="tts-word">pazarlık</span> <span data-word-idx="231" class="tts-word">konusu</span> <span data-word-idx="232" class="tts-word">olamaz&rdquo;</span></span></strong></p>
<p style="margin-bottom:1.5rem"><span><span data-word-idx="233" class="tts-word">Silopi</span> <span data-word-idx="234" class="tts-word">Dayanışma</span> <span data-word-idx="235" class="tts-word">İnisiyatifi</span> <span data-word-idx="236" class="tts-word">a&ccedil;ıklamasında,</span> <span data-word-idx="237" class="tts-word">&ldquo;Bizler,</span> <span data-word-idx="238" class="tts-word">halkın</span> <span data-word-idx="239" class="tts-word">iradesini</span> <span data-word-idx="240" class="tts-word">koruma</span> <span data-word-idx="241" class="tts-word">ve</span> <span data-word-idx="242" class="tts-word">demokrasiye</span> <span data-word-idx="243" class="tts-word">sahip</span> <span data-word-idx="244" class="tts-word">&ccedil;ıkma</span> <span data-word-idx="245" class="tts-word">g&ouml;revimizi</span> <span data-word-idx="246" class="tts-word">s&uuml;rd&uuml;receğiz.</span> <span data-word-idx="247" class="tts-word">Silopi&rsquo;nin</span> <span data-word-idx="248" class="tts-word">onuru,</span> <span data-word-idx="249" class="tts-word">&ouml;zg&uuml;rl&uuml;ğ&uuml;</span> <span data-word-idx="250" class="tts-word">ve</span> <span data-word-idx="251" class="tts-word">iradesi</span> <span data-word-idx="252" class="tts-word">hi&ccedil;bir</span> <span data-word-idx="253" class="tts-word">g&uuml;&ccedil;,</span> <span data-word-idx="254" class="tts-word">&ccedil;ıkar</span> <span data-word-idx="255" class="tts-word">ya</span> <span data-word-idx="256" class="tts-word">da</span> <span data-word-idx="257" class="tts-word">g&uuml;ndelik</span> <span data-word-idx="258" class="tts-word">hesap</span> <span data-word-idx="259" class="tts-word">uğruna</span> <span data-word-idx="260" class="tts-word">feda</span> <span data-word-idx="261" class="tts-word">edilemez&rdquo;</span> <span data-word-idx="262" class="tts-word">ifadelerine</span> <span data-word-idx="263" class="tts-word">yer</span> <span data-word-idx="264" class="tts-word">verdi.</span></span></p>
<p style="margin-bottom:1.5rem"><strong><span><span data-word-idx="265" class="tts-word">Ne</span> <span data-word-idx="266" class="tts-word">olmuştu?</span></span></strong></p>
<p style="margin-bottom:1.5rem"><span><span data-word-idx="267" class="tts-word">DEM</span> <span data-word-idx="268" class="tts-word">Parti,</span> <span data-word-idx="269" class="tts-word">Jiyan</span> <span data-word-idx="270" class="tts-word">Ormanlı&rsquo;yı</span> <span data-word-idx="271" class="tts-word">&ldquo;eşbaşkanlık</span> <span data-word-idx="272" class="tts-word">sistemine</span> <span data-word-idx="273" class="tts-word">karşı</span> <span data-word-idx="274" class="tts-word">tutum</span> <span data-word-idx="275" class="tts-word">almak,</span> <span data-word-idx="276" class="tts-word">ortak</span> <span data-word-idx="277" class="tts-word">y&ouml;netim</span> <span data-word-idx="278" class="tts-word">anlayışını</span> <span data-word-idx="279" class="tts-word">benimsememek,</span> <span data-word-idx="280" class="tts-word">parti</span> <span data-word-idx="281" class="tts-word">kararlarını</span> <span data-word-idx="282" class="tts-word">tanımamak</span> <span data-word-idx="283" class="tts-word">ve</span> <span data-word-idx="284" class="tts-word">halkın</span> <span data-word-idx="285" class="tts-word">beklentilerini</span> <span data-word-idx="286" class="tts-word">karşılamamak&rdquo;</span> <span data-word-idx="287" class="tts-word">gerek&ccedil;eleriyle</span> <span data-word-idx="288" class="tts-word">Merkez</span> <span data-word-idx="289" class="tts-word">Disiplin</span> <span data-word-idx="290" class="tts-word">Kurulu&rsquo;na</span> <span data-word-idx="291" class="tts-word">sevk</span> <span data-word-idx="292" class="tts-word">ettiğini</span> <span data-word-idx="293" class="tts-word">a&ccedil;ıklamıştı.</span></span></p>
<p style="margin-bottom:1.5rem"><span><span data-word-idx="294" class="tts-word">Parti</span> <span data-word-idx="295" class="tts-word">tarafından</span> <span data-word-idx="296" class="tts-word">yapılan</span> <span data-word-idx="297" class="tts-word">a&ccedil;ıklamada,</span> <span data-word-idx="298" class="tts-word">Ormanlı&rsquo;nın</span> <span data-word-idx="299" class="tts-word">&ldquo;DEM</span> <span data-word-idx="300" class="tts-word">Parti&rsquo;nin</span> <span data-word-idx="301" class="tts-word">yerel</span> <span data-word-idx="302" class="tts-word">y&ouml;netim</span> <span data-word-idx="303" class="tts-word">politikalarıyla</span> <span data-word-idx="304" class="tts-word">&ouml;rt&uuml;şmeyen</span> <span data-word-idx="305" class="tts-word">uygulamalarda</span> <span data-word-idx="306" class="tts-word">bulunduğu&rdquo;</span> <span data-word-idx="307" class="tts-word">ileri</span> <span data-word-idx="308" class="tts-word">s&uuml;r&uuml;lm&uuml;ş,</span> <span data-word-idx="309" class="tts-word">bu</span> <span data-word-idx="310" class="tts-word">nedenlerle</span> <span data-word-idx="311" class="tts-word">&ldquo;kesin</span> <span data-word-idx="312" class="tts-word">ihra&ccedil;&rdquo;</span> <span data-word-idx="313" class="tts-word">talep</span> <span data-word-idx="314" class="tts-word">edilmişti.</span></span></p>
<p style="margin-bottom:1.5rem"><strong><span><span data-word-idx="315" class="tts-word">Ormanlı:</span> <span data-word-idx="316" class="tts-word">G&ouml;revimin</span> <span data-word-idx="317" class="tts-word">başındayım</span></span></strong></p>
